```ts
/**
 * 左侧栏的页面清单。
 *
 * 把「有哪些页」抽成纯数据，理由与 help.ts 一样：导航是产品结构的一部分，
 * 少一页、多一页、改个名字都应该在测试里红，而不是等用户找不着功能。
 * 页面 id 同时是 data-shot 的后缀（nav-home、page-home），点测靠它认路。
 */
export type PageId = 'home' | 'intake' | 'interview' | 'report' | 'history' | 'settings';

export interface PageItem {
  id: PageId;
  label: string;
  /** 一句话说明这一页管什么，鼠标悬停与向导都会用到 */
  blurb: string;
}

export const PAGE_ITEMS: PageItem[] = [
  { id: 'home', label: '首页', blurb: '这是什么、从哪开始' },
  { id: 'intake', label: '投料台', blurb: '贴简历与岗位、挑面试官、开始质询' },
  { id: 'interview', label: '质询台', blurb: '实时过程、主张与判定、原文与改写' },
  { id: 'report', label: '报告', blurb: '五种带走方式与成长档案' },
  { id: 'history', label: '历史', blurb: '跑过的每一场与跨场次仪表盘' },
  { id: 'settings', label: '设置', blurb: '配色、隐私、启动方式' },
];

export const DEFAULT_PAGE: PageId = 'home';

/** 认不出的值一律回首页：页面 id 可能来自旧链接或手改的地址，不该白屏 */
export function pageOf(value: unknown): PageId {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return DEFAULT_PAGE;
  const found = PAGE_ITEMS.find((item) => item.id === value);
  return found === undefined ? DEFAULT_PAGE : found.id;
}
```

**SideNav.tsx**:
```tsx
import { PAGE_ITEMS } from '../pages.ts';
import type { PageId } from '../pages.ts';

export function SideNav(props: {
  page: PageId;
  onPick: (page: PageId) => void;
  /** 每页右上角的小角标：例如历史有几场、质询台有没有跑过 */
  badges?: Partial<Record<PageId, string>>;
  className?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <nav className={'flex shrink-0 flex-col gap-1 border-r border-ink-800 bg-ink-950/60 p-2 xl:w-[176px] ' + (props.className ?? '')} data-shot="nav" aria-label="页面">
      {PAGE_ITEMS.map(item => {
        const active = item.id === props.page;
        const badge = props.badges?.[item.id];
        return (
          <button key={item.id} type="button" data-shot={'nav-' + item.id} title={item.blurb}
            aria-current={active ? 'page' : undefined}
            className={'flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-[12.5px] transition-colors ' + (active ? 'bg-ember-500/10 text-ember-200 border border-ember-500/40' : 'border border-transparent text-ink-300 hover:border-ink-700 hover:text-ink-100')}
            onClick={() => props.onPick(item.id)}>
            <span className="flex-1 truncate">{item.label}</span>
            {badge === undefined ? null : <span className="font-mono text-[10.5px] text-ink-500">{badge}</span>}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}
```
On small screens the nav should be horizontal row. Use responsive classes: `flex-row overflow-x-auto xl:flex-col`. Fine.

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { WIZARD_STEPS } from '../help.ts';
import type { PageId } from '../pages.ts';

export function OnboardingWizard(props: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  /** 走到某一步时先切到它该去的页面，让文字与背后的界面是同一件事 */
  onPage: (page: PageId) => void;
}) {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const step = WIZARD_STEPS[index] ?? WIZARD_STEPS[0];
  useEffect(() => { if (props.open) setIndex(0); }, [props.open]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!props.open) return;
    const page = step?.page;
    if (page !== undefined) props.onPage(page);
  }, [props.open, index]);
  if (!props.open || step === undefined) return null;
  ...
}
```

```
/** 从地址栏的 #/xxx 读页名。读不出来就是首页（含没有 hash 的情况）。 */
function readPageFromHash(): PageId {
  if (typeof location === 'undefined') return DEFAULT_PAGE;
  return pageOf(location.hash.replace(/^#\/?/, ''));
}
```

handlePickPage deps [] — uses setPage only plus location. 
```ts
  /** 切页：同时把页名写进地址栏，刷新后停在同一页，也能把某一页直接发链接给别人 */
  const handlePickPage = useCallback((next: PageId) => {
    setPage(next);
    if (typeof location !== 'undefined') location.hash = '/' + next;
  }, []);

  // 浏览器前进/后退与手改地址栏都要跟着换页：地址栏是这一页的唯一来源
  useEffect(() => {
    const onHash = () => setPage(readPageFromHash());
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', onHash);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', onHash);
  }, []);

  const handleCloseWizard = useCallback(() => {
    setWizardOpen(false);
    markWizardSeen();
  }, []);
```

- SQLite 坑（重要）：ALTER TABLE session RENAME TO session_v9 会把子表的 REFERENCES session 一起改写成 session_v9 → 表重建必须用「建 session_next → INSERT SELECT → DROP session → RENAME session_next TO session」。
- 报告加「比上次」一节（report.ts compareLines + report-html.ts compareSection），章节编号整体 +1：renumber/bumpNumeral 只认 '## ' 前缀，第一版对整段取首字符导致不生效。

Now implement app-window mode: find a Chromium-family browser (Edge/Chrome) and launch with `--app=<url> --window-size=...`. Zero dependency: search common paths on Windows, macOS, Linux.

Design (keep testable):
- `appWindowCandidates(platform, env)` → list of absolute paths to check, in order.
- `browserOpenArgs(url, platform, opts)` — extend to accept `{ appPath }`: if appPath given → `{ command: appPath, args: ['--app=' + url, '--window-size=1440,960'] }`; else fallback to default browser.
- `chooseBrowser(platform, env, exists)` pure function: takes a predicate `exists(path)` so tests can stub; returns path or null.

Windows candidates:
- env.ProgramFiles(x86) etc. Better: use env vars: `ProgramFiles`, `ProgramFiles(x86)`, `LOCALAPPDATA`.
  - `%ProgramFiles(x86)%\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe`
  - `%ProgramFiles%\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe`
  - `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe`
  - `%ProgramFiles%\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe`
  - `%ProgramFiles(x86)%\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe`
  - `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe`
  - `%ProgramFiles%\BraveSoftware\Brave-Browser\Application\brave.exe`
darwin:
  - '/Applications/Microsoft Edge.app/Contents/MacOS/Microsoft Edge'
  - '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'
  - '/Applications/Brave Browser.app/Contents/MacOS/Brave Browser'
linux: 'microsoft-edge', 'google-chrome', 'chromium', '/usr/bin/...'

Also the app-window flow: with `--app=`, we need a user-data-dir? No, using existing profile is fine but may conflict with a running Edge instance — `--app` on a running Edge will open a new window in the existing process, that's fine and actually good (reuses profile). But if Edge's first-run profile is not set up, `--app` still works.

Hmm, one caveat: if Edge is running with existing profile, `--app=URL` opens the app window in that instance. Good.

Also we should give a stable `--user-data-dir`? No — avoid, since reusing the default profile is fine and avoids a fresh first-run experience.

Additional flag: `--window-size=1440,960` and maybe `--no-first-run`.

The DB is empty now — so all sessions WERE deleted server-side. Then why did `finalList.length === 1`?

`finalList = await sessions()` — let me check `sessions()` in e2e: probably fetch `/api/sessions` returning `{sessions:[...]}` or list. Wait maybe `sessions()` returns `.sessions` and the count of 1 is... Hmm but DB is empty → /api/sessions should return [].

Unless: `sessions()` calls with a cache-buster? Or the server had 1 session in *memory*? No.

Hold on — maybe after the loop, the LAST delete's request was still in flight when `sessions()` was called (optimistic removal made delCount 0, breaking the loop), and `sessions()` hit the server before the DELETE committed? But the DB is empty at the end, so the delete did commit — just after our GET. That's the race!

Sequence: loop i=1 click (last row) → optimistic removal → delCount 0 → break → immediately `sessions()` GET. But this happens ~immediately after the click (no wait), so the DELETE may not have been processed yet → GET returns the still-present session (1) → FAIL. Then later the DELETE commits → DB empty.

But wait, iteration i=0 also had this race, and its waitFor also resolved from optimistic removal. Actually the check for i=0 didn't verify the server. Only the final `sessions()` check. So yes — the loop's waitFor watches the DOM, which updates optimistically, so the loop can outrun the server; the final check then reads the server too early.

Real fix in e2e: the final assertion should wait for the server list to be empty, e.g. `const finalList = await waitForServerSessionsEmpty()` with retry up to ~10s. That's legitimate: the UI is optimistic-by-design (documented), so "server converged" needs a wait.

Better yet: make the loop wait on the *server* count decreasing, which is the true convergence. But DOM-based is also fine since API is the ground truth for the final check. I'll change the final check to poll the server:

```js
const finalList = await waitFor(async () => { const l = await sessions(); return l.length === 0 ? l : ''; }, '服务端历史清空', 12000);
```
But waitFor throws on timeout. There's an existing helper `waitSessionsBelow(n, ms)` used earlier: returns list when below. So use `await waitSessionsBelow(0, 12000)` → returns list when length <= -1? Let me check waitSessionsBelow implementation: probably `n < target`? Fix: the loop should confirm server-side decrease (not just DOM), and/or the final check should poll the server.

Cleanest minimal change:
- In the loop, replace the DOM-based waitFor with a wait on the server list: wait until `sessions()` length < serverBefore (read server before the click).
- Final check: `const finalList = await waitSessionsBelow(0, 12000);` — that returns as soon as length<=0, i.e. empty; if it times out it returns the current list and check fails with detail.

But careful: `waitSessionsBelow(0, ...)` returns list of length 0 only if empty; if it's already 0 it returns immediately. Good.

I'll implement:
```js
for (let i = 0; i < 6; i += 1) {
  const serverBefore = (await sessions()).length;
  if (serverBefore === 0) break;
  const beforeRows = await delCount();
  if (beforeRows === 0) break;
  await evalJs('... click ...');
  await waitFor(... delCount() < beforeRows ..., '列表少一行', 12000);
  await waitSessionsBelow(serverBefore - 1, 12000);
}
const finalList = await waitSessionsBelow(0, 12000);
```

That covers it robustly.

Add:
```js
/** 应用窗口的尺寸：够放下四栏（投料台 / 主张与追问 / 原文 / 过程面板）。 */
export const APP_WINDOW_SIZE = '1440,960';

/**
 * 无地址栏「应用窗口」的候选浏览器。
 * 优先 Edge（Windows 一定有），再 Chrome / Brave；
 * macOS 与 Linux 给的是可执行文件的惯常位置与命令名。
 */
export function appBrowserCandidates(platform = process.platform, env = process.env) {
  const pf = env['ProgramFiles'] ?? 'C:/Program Files';
  const pf86 = env['ProgramFiles(x86)'] ?? 'C:/Program Files (x86)';
  const local = env['LOCALAPPDATA'] ?? '';
  if (platform === 'win32') {
    return [
      join(pf86, 'Microsoft', 'Edge', 'Application', 'msedge.exe'),
      join(pf, 'Microsoft', 'Edge', 'Application', 'msedge.exe'),
      join(local, 'Microsoft', 'Edge', 'Application', 'msedge.exe'),
      join(pf, 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe'),
      join(pf86, 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe'),
      join(local, 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe'),
      join(pf, 'BraveSoftware', 'Brave-Browser', 'Application', 'brave.exe'),
    ].filter((p) => p !== '' && p.indexOf('//') < 0);
  }
  ...
}
```
Careful: if `local` is '' then join('' , 'Microsoft', ...) → 'Microsoft/Edge/...' relative — filter out by checking local !== ''.

Simpler: build arrays per platform with explicit checks.

```js
export function appBrowserCandidates(platform = process.platform, env = process.env) {
  const list = [];
  const push = (base, tail) => { if (typeof base === 'string' && base.trim() !== '') list.push(join(base, ...tail)); };
  if (platform === 'win32') {
    const pf = env['ProgramFiles'];
    const pf86 = env['ProgramFiles(x86)'];
    const local = env['LOCALAPPDATA'];
    for (const base of [pf86, pf, local]) {
      push(base, ['Microsoft', 'Edge', 'Application', 'msedge.exe']);
      push(base, ['Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe']);
    }
    push(pf, ['BraveSoftware', 'Brave-Browser', 'Application', 'brave.exe']);
    push(pf86, ['BraveSoftware', 'Brave-Browser', 'Application', 'brave.exe']);
  } else if (platform === 'darwin') {
    list.push('/Applications/Microsoft Edge.app/Contents/MacOS/Microsoft Edge');
    list.push('/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome');
    list.push('/Applications/Brave Browser.app/Contents/MacOS/Brave Browser');
  } else {
    list.push('/usr/bin/microsoft-edge', '/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/usr/bin/chromium', '/usr/bin/chromium-browser');
  }
  return list;
}

/** 找到第一个真的存在的候选。exists 由调用方注入（纯函数不碰文件系统）。 */
export function chooseAppBrowser(platform = process.platform, env = process.env, exists = () => false) {
  for (const path of appBrowserCandidates(platform, env)) {
    if (exists(path)) return path;
  }
  return null;
}

/** 有没有地址栏的两种开法：给了浏览器就用应用窗口，没给就退回系统默认浏览器。 */
export function browserOpenArgs(url, platform = process.platform, appPath = null) {
  if (typeof appPath === 'string' && appPath.trim() !== '') {
    return { command: appPath, args: ['--app=' + url, '--window-size=' + APP_WINDOW_SIZE] };
  }
  if (platform === 'win32') return { command: 'cmd', args: ['/c', 'start', '', url] };
  if (platform === 'darwin') return { command: 'open', args: [url] };
  return { command: 'xdg-open', args: [url] };
}
```

```js
describe('应用窗口', () => {
  const env = { ProgramFiles: 'C:/PF', 'ProgramFiles(x86)': 'C:/PF86', LOCALAPPDATA: 'C:/Local' };

  it('Windows 的候选里 Edge 排在最前，且都是从环境变量推出来的绝对路径', () => {
    const list = appBrowserCandidates('win32', env);
    expect(list[0]).toBe(join('C:/PF86', 'Microsoft', 'Edge', 'Application', 'msedge.exe'));
    expect(list).toContain(join('C:/PF', 'Microsoft', 'Edge', 'Application', 'msedge.exe'));
    expect(list).toContain(join('C:/Local', 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe'));
  });

  it('环境变量缺了就不编路径——宁可没有候选，也不要一条点不动的命令', () => {
    const list = appBrowserCandidates('win32', {});
    expect(list).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('取第一个真的存在的那个；一个都没有时返回 null，由调用方退回默认浏览器', () => {
    const missing = chooseAppBrowser('win32', env, () => false);
    expect(missing).toBeNull();
    const hit = join('C:/PF', 'Google', 'Chrome', 'Application', 'chrome.exe');
    expect(chooseAppBrowser('win32', env, (p) => p === hit)).toBe(hit);
  });

  it('macOS 与 Linux 给的是惯常位置', () => {
    expect(appBrowserCandidates('darwin', {})).toContain('/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome');
    expect(appBrowserCandidates('linux', {})).toContain('/usr/bin/google-chrome');
  });

  it('给了浏览器就用没有地址栏的应用窗口，尺寸带上；没给就还是老三样', () => {
    const app = browserOpenArgs('http://127.0.0.1:8787', 'win32', 'C:/PF/msedge.exe');
    expect(app.command).toBe('C:/PF/msedge.exe');
    expect(app.args).toEqual(['--app=http://127.0.0.1:8787', '--window-size=' + APP_WINDOW_SIZE]);
    expect(browserOpenArgs('http://127.0.0.1:8787', 'win32', '')).toEqual({ command: 'cmd', args: ['/c', 'start', '', 'http://127.0.0.1:8787'] });
  });
});
```

```js
function openBrowser(url) {
  if (process.env['CROSSEXAM_LAUNCH_NO_BROWSER'] === '1') { ... }
  // 先找一个能开「应用窗口」的浏览器（没有地址栏、没有标签页），找不到就退回系统默认浏览器：
  // 宁可给一个有地址栏的窗口，也不要让人对着一个报错框发呆。
  const app = chooseAppBrowser(process.platform, process.env, existsSync);
  const plan = browserOpenArgs(url, process.platform, app);
  say(app === null ? '  用系统默认浏览器打开（没找到 Edge / Chrome，窗口会带地址栏）' : '  用 ' + app + ' 开一个没有地址栏的应用窗口');
  spawn(plan.command, plan.args, { stdio: 'ignore', detached: true, shell: false }).unref();
}
```
